Task mobile-m3-rating-writer: complete (this commit, base 9f7b18eced, closed the last known gap of its class by taking the star tap off the main thread: `RatingWriter` is one activity-owned thread that writes one tap at a time and hands the outcome back through the caller's own main-thread hop, so `PlaybackControls.setRating` traded its return value for a callback and nothing else changed — the star still moves only after the database agreed, is never moved optimistically and never reverted, and a refusal still arrives as the self-dismissing `TransientMessage`; a queued write was the wrong thing to fuse into `play_recorder.rs`, whose `PlayRecorder` belongs to the playback service's own `Db` handle and not to the activity's `MusicLibrary`, so "one connection and one queue" would have meant either a rating that stops working whenever playback is unbound or a second recorder, plus a UniFFI callback out of a Rust thread and a discrete must-answer write parked behind up to twenty seconds of SQLITE_BUSY back-off per queued play; the pinned `failedRatingShowsTheErrorWithoutMovingTheStars` was proven to still bite by moving the star before the answer, which reddened it and the new `theStarsWaitForTheWriteToAnswerAndOnlyThenMove` with "Failed to assert the following: (StateDescription = 'Rated 2 of 5')" before the mutation was removed and both went green; `library/stats.rs` now documents what is actually the case — the split from `scanner.rs` is about what is written, not which thread writes it, and every helper has to be callable from any thread holding its own handle, with the desktop's UI thread, Android's play-count writer, Android's rating writer and tag writeback all named; also found and recorded in the record: a rating tapped during a scan used to block the main thread on the `MusicLibrary` mutex for the whole SAF folder walk, and now waits off it; format, strict workspace Clippy, `reprise-core --lib` at 2045 passed with 0 failed and 1 ignored, `reprise-android-ffi` at 38 passed, Android theme lint and native Android build passed; Task play-11-immediate-continuation: complete (this commit, base 2c6343dbd4, moved PLAY-11's escape hatch out of `up_next_transport.rs` into the new `playback/library_continuation.rs` so both moments it can be reached ask one shared gate, `cleared_library_filter_handoff`, instead of carrying two copies of "may this snapshot escape into the full library?"; an exhausted, filter-born Music snapshot now takes its random full-library continuation the moment Music becomes completely unfiltered, keeping the running title at the cursor, unrestarted and queued exactly once, so the queue stops reading "Queue is empty" for the rest of that title, while the end-of-title handoff stays for a filter cleared too early to help; # Mobile M8 — a counted play survives the process

Task mobile-m8-durable-play-journal: complete (this commit, base d4dfc5ebb1, put one bounded private-file journal underneath the existing session-owned play writer instead of adding a second counting path: the worker appends the track id, event-time timestamp and monotonic sequence before its database attempt, Core schema v54 stores one applied high-water and commits it in the same immediate transaction as the existing `_in` count update, a post-commit pre-removal replay is therefore a no-op, malformed complete lines and the expected half-written tail are named and repaired while earlier valid entries replay, and scanner contention retains the unchanged four attempts and exponential backoff before leaving the named entry durable for a later drain; the 1,024-entry cap is about 65 KiB and explicitly refuses the newest play because evicting an older already-promised entry would be the less honest loss; mutation runs produced the real expected reds — disabled startup drain left count 0 instead of 1, weakened `<=` duplicate suppression produced count 2 instead of 1, discarding valid rows during corruption repair left count 0 instead of 1, a 150 ms caller-side stall failed the playback-thread bound, a shortened retry schedule lost the 1-second fourth offer, separating the count from the high-water transaction left count 1 instead of rolling back to 0, and invalid singleton seeding failed the v53 migration — and every restored focused test passed; final format and strict workspace Clippy passed, isolated four-thread Core passed 2,059/0/1 ignored, Android FFI passed 47/0, Android theme lint and the two-ABI native build passed, and the fresh Gradle result directory contained nine XML files all at mtime 1785884842 after start 1785884810 and summed to 73 tests with zero failures, errors or skips before debug APK assembly passed and the daemon was explicitly stopped; The default filter row was also confirmed in a real headless screenshot after an earlier capture proved to be a stale window from a leftover Xvfb — the code had been right and the picture wrong, which is why two tests now pin it instead)

# Delete responsiveness
